Modern PRNG implementations may be initialized through either a seed or, more accurately, an initial state that exceeds the capacity of a conventional integer seed. However, reliance on a simple seed alone frequently proves insufficient to ensure consistent program execution traces across different implementations. A natural assumption is that transferring the complete internal state of a generator should guarantee identical outputs regardless of the software library used. This study examines the validity of this assumption by investigating whether complete initial states can ensure cross-library fidelity and portability of PRNG streams. We focus on two widely deployed generators, Mersenne Twister and Philox, and evaluate their implementations across four major Python ecosystems-Random, NumPy, PyTorch, and TensorFlow. We compare the sequences produced by these implementations against those generated by the original reference algorithms under identical initialization conditions. Our results demonstrate that reproducibility cannot be assumed from PRNG state transfer alone, even when implementations claim to follow the same underlying algorithm. While fidelity was successfully achieved for several implementations, significant discrepancies were observed in others. Most notably, the Philox implementation in PyTorch exhibits fundamental incompatibilities with the reference algorithm, preventing exact reproduction of generator outputs across environments. These findings challenge the common expectation that access to a full internal state of a PRNG is sufficient to ensure reproducibility across software stacks. They further highlight that implementation-specific design choices can introduce hidden barriers to experimental replication, particularly in AI workflows that rely on multiple frameworks. This work shows that implementation fidelity of a PRNG is a necessary condition for scientific reproducibility and makes two primary contributions. First, it identifies practical guidelines for achieving reliable PRNG usage and reproducibility within the Python scientific and AI ecosystem. Second, it evaluates the extent to which cross-library portability and fidelity can be recovered through user-level techniques, without requiring modifications to library source code.

</details>


### [282] [REFLEX with Jev for Efficient Selective Control in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26532)
*Tiantong Wu,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出REFLEX架构，结合快速类型化决策层Jev与强LLM，在保持95%成功率的同时减少72.7%的强模型调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM代理在使用生成模型进行有限决策时的效率问题，探索在不降低任务成功率的前提下如何更高效地处理决策。

Method: 构建REFLEX架构，使用Jev作为快速、类型化的决策层；当置信度低或需要生成时调用强LLM。在100个任务的冻结基准上进行评估，并进行控制干预分析可靠性因素。

Result: REFLEX在100任务基准上实现95%成功率，相比仅使用强模型的代理减少72.7%的强模型调用；可靠性受动作集大小和授权边界附近有效替代方案的影响；与低成本生成级联相比，在路由已高度准确时优势有限。

Conclusion: 选择性控制结合Jev可在特定条件下显著减少计算开销，但其收益在常规路由已高精度的场景中受限，明确了该方法的适用边界。

Abstract: LLM agents often use generative models for bounded decisions, raising the question of when these decisions can be handled more efficiently without reducing task success. We study REFLEX, an agent architecture that uses Jev as a fast, typed decision layer and calls a strong LLM when confidence is low, or generation is required. On a frozen 100-task benchmark, REFLEX achieves 95% success with 72.7% fewer strong-model calls than a strong-only agent, with reductions persisting across three fallback families. Controlled interventions show that reliability depends on action-set size and near-valid alternatives near authorization boundaries. External BFCL and $τ$-style evaluations reveal limited advantages over a cheap generative cascade when ordinary routing is already highly accurate. These findings identify when selective control with Jev can reduce computation and where its benefits are limited.

</details>


### [283] [JEV-as-a-Judge: Accept When Confident, Escalate When Unsure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26550)
*Yubo Li,Yidi Miao,Ramayya Krishnan,Rema Padman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为JEV的“仅决策”评估模型，作为LLM裁判的经济型初筛方案。通过冻结级联机制（接受高置信度判决，升级低置信度案例），在保持99%准确率的同时，将成本降至顶级LLM裁判的0.36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型作为裁判（LLM-as-a-judge）虽能支持多样化任务评估，但在大规模应用时面临推理成本高和置信度可靠性不足的关键挑战。旨在探索是否存在一种更经济的“仅决策”裁判能提供高效的初步筛选，并准确识别何时需要更强力的评估介入。

Method: 将JEV裁判与16种生成式及奖励模型裁判进行对比，采用盲测人类裁决作为基准。研究重点在于比较不同裁判在普通偏好判断和基于证据的事实性判断上的表现，分析其在处理推导检查或抵抗精心构造的错误答案时的差异，以及JEV置信度分布对性能的影响。

Result: JEV在普通偏好和事实性判断任务上，性能与最先进的LLM裁判相差不到3个百分点，但费用仅为后者的0.36%。主要差距出现在需要复杂推导或对抗性强的场景中，且这些差距主要集中在低置信度的决策中。构建的冻结级联策略（结合JEV初筛与高阶裁判复核）成功保留了99%的准确率。

Conclusion: JEV作为一种低成本、高效率的初筛工具是可行的。通过置信度驱动的级联评估机制，可以在显著降低计算成本的同时，维持接近顶级模型的评估准确性，为解决大规模LLM评估中的成本与可靠性平衡问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge enables evaluation across diverse tasks, but inference cost and confidence reliability become critical at scale. We study whether a decision-only judge can provide an economical first pass and identify when stronger evaluation is needed. Comparing jev-as-a-judge with sixteen generative and reward-model judges, with blinded human adjudication, we find it within three percentage points of a state-of-the-art LLM judge, our strongest comparator, on ordinary preference and evidence-grounded factuality at 0.36% of the comparator's fee. Larger gaps arise when judgments require checking a derivation or resisting an elaborately written wrong answer. On several benchmarks, JEV's gap to this comparator is concentrated in low-confidence decisions. A frozen cascade that accepts confident verdicts and escalates uncertain ones retains 99% of the comparator's accuracy at lower cost.

</details>


### [284] [Neutral-Atom-based Quantum Optimization for Resource Allocation in NOMA Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26556)
*Patatchona Keyela,Remon Polus,Soumaya Cherkaoui,Ola Ahmad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用中性原子量子计算平台解决无线通信网络中的最大接入问题（MAP），通过将混合整数规划转化为图论中的最大独立集（MIS）问题，利用Rydberg原子阵列的物理特性进行编码，验证了该方法在大规模资源优化中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信网络中的资源优化问题通常属于NP难问题，具有组合性质和高计算复杂度。尽管基于中性原子的量子计算在处理此类问题上展现出潜力，但其在无线通信优化领域的应用尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1. 将最大接入问题（MAP）建模为混合整数规划任务，涵盖非正交多址接入（NOMA）上行链路网络中的准入控制、用户聚类、信道分配和功率分配。
2. 将MAP等价重构为图论中的最大独立集（MIS）问题以降低计算负担。
3. 利用基于Rydberg原子阵列的中性原子量子平台，将MIS问题自然编码到系统的物理几何结构和阻塞约束中。

Result: 数值结果证明了该方法在解决大规模无线资源优化问题方面的可行性和潜力。

Conclusion: 中性原子量子平台为处理无线通信中复杂的NP难资源优化问题提供了一种有效且可行的新途径。

Abstract: In wireless communication networks, many resource optimization problems are nondeterministic polynomial-time hard (NP-hard) due to their combinatorial nature and high computational complexity. Recently, neutral-atom-based quantum computing has emerged as a promising platform for efficiently solving such problems by leveraging quantum superposition and entanglement. However, its application to wireless communication optimization problems remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate the use of neutral-atom quantum platforms to solve the maximum access problem (MAP), formulated as a mixed-integer programming task that jointly considers admission control, user clustering, channel assignment, and power allocation in a non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA)-enabled uplink network. To reduce the computational burden, the MAP is equivalently reformulated as a maximum independent set (MIS) problem in graph theory. This reformulation enables the use of the neutral atom platform based on Rydberg atom arrays, where the MIS problem is naturally encoded into the physical geometry and blockade constraints of the quantum system. Numerical results demonstrate the feasibility and potential of this approach for addressing large-scale wireless resource optimization problems.

</details>


### [285] [Quantum-Aided Active Device Detection in Energy-Harvesting Symbiotic Radio Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26565)
*Remon Polus,Deemah Tashman,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合能量收集、码域NOMA和LDS编码的共生无线电系统，利用Grover量子搜索算法解决上行链路活跃设备检测问题，在保持接近最大似然性能的同时显著降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代网络中大规模物联网部署需要高能效和频谱效率的解决方案；上行链路共生无线电中的活跃设备检测直接影响解码可靠性、干扰管理和系统吞吐量，且传统方法复杂度高。

Method: 构建能量收集码域非正交多接入共生无线电系统，IoT设备通过低密度扩频码从环境上行信号中获取能量并反向散射信息；采用Grover量子搜索算法进行活跃设备检测，以替代传统的穷举最大似然搜索。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提方法在大幅减少搜索迭代次数的同时，性能紧密逼近最大似然估计结果。

Conclusion: 该方法有效降低了活跃设备检测的计算复杂度，展示了其在可扩展的环境感知物联网系统中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Massive connectivity in next-generation networks demands energy- and spectrum-efficient solutions for large-scale Internet of Things (IoT) deployments. Symbiotic radio (SR) enables passive IoT devices to communicate by backscattering existing cellular transmissions. A key challenge in uplink SR is active device detection (ADD), which directly affects decoding reliability, interference management, and system throughput. We propose an energy-harvesting code-domain non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA)-SR system in which IoT devices harvest energy from ambient uplink signals and backscatter information using low-density spreading (LDS) codes. To reduce the complexity of ADD, Grover's quantum search algorithm is employed, providing a quadratic reduction in oracle-query complexity over exhaustive maximum-likelihood (ML) search. Numerical results show that the proposed approach closely approaches ML performance while substantially reducing the number of search iterations, demonstrating its potential for scalable ambient IoT systems.

</details>


### [286] [The Delegation Blind Spot: Auditing Product Decisions from Agent Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26642)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于决策的审计方法，通过映射观测通道和产品价值对比到兼容区间及见证群体，以评估模型在特定任务中的决策不确定性。研究发现保守区间多未解析，而确定性提取器无需模型调用即可解决多数比较，揭示了模型报告引入的不必要性，并建议保留结构化输入以诊断决策未决原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决智能体执行中因未来产品改进价值不明确导致的决策不确定性问题，旨在提供一种可执行的测量工作流来识别和量化这种不确定性，而非仅仅依赖更多的遥测数据。

Method: 1. 建立基于识别理论和决策理论的审计基础，将声明的观测通道和产品价值对比映射为兼容区间和见证群体。2. 进行冻结实验：向两个固定的模型快照发送4,800次请求，处理共享的合成任务。3. 探索性跟进：记录2,400次调用的用户偏好以解决部分比较。4. 使用确定性提取器在无模型调用情况下进行分析。5. 进行14,400次受控多项式模拟，区分结构歧义、弱识别和有限校准精度。6. 提出带源标签的决策收据和离线查看器。

Result: 1. 所有36个保守主要区间在模型执行准确率不同的情况下仍未解析。2. 探索性研究仅每模型解决了9次比较中的3次。3. 确定性提取器在无模型调用或校准观察的情况下解决了9次比较中的7次，暴露出模型生成报告引入了不必要的 uncertainty。4. 模拟成功区分了结构歧义与弱识别/有限校准精度。

Conclusion: 应保留与决策相关的结构化输入，并在收集更多遥测数据之前诊断决策为何未决。该研究强调了通过审计而非单纯增加数据量来解决决策不确定性的有效性，且所有分析均可复现。

Abstract: Successful agent execution need not identify which future product improvement its user would value. We present a decision-specific audit that maps a declared observation channel and product-value contrast to compatible intervals and witness populations. Its foundations are established identification and decision theory; the contribution is an executable measurement workflow and a controlled study of its limits. A frozen experiment makes 4,800 requests to two pinned model snapshots on shared synthetic tasks. All 36 conservative primary intervals remain unresolved despite different execution accuracy. An exploratory 2,400-call follow-up records supplied preferences and resolves three of nine comparisons per model. A deterministic extractor resolves seven of nine without model calls or calibration observations, exposing unnecessary uncertainty introduced by model-generated reports. A further 14,400 controlled multinomial simulations distinguish structural ambiguity from weak identification and finite calibration precision. We propose a source-labeled decision receipt and provide an offline viewer for inspecting the audit. These results motivate preserving decision-relevant structured input and diagnosing why a decision is unresolved before collecting more telemetry. The study contains no human participants or real customer outcomes. Full proofs, raw model provenance, controlled experiments, and reproducible analyses accompany the report.

</details>


### [287] [Type-Safe Is Not Error-Free: A Constrained Decision Head Follows the Option Name, Not the Rubric Bound to It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26758)
*Yu Sun,Junhao Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示，在结构化决策模型中，选项名称的语义极性（如0/1与no/yes）会系统性扭曲模型输出，导致决策排名反转，但不会增加类型错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证已构建的结构化决策模型是否真正按预期解释预定义选项，尽管其输出符合模式要求，但需确认模型对选项含义的理解是否与人类意图一致。

Method: 通过改变选项名称与文本规范（rubrics）的对应关系（例如将0/1重命名为no/yes），保持其他条件不变，测试Jev等具有开放权重的模型在1200个工作流决策中的表现。

Result: 重命名含语义极性的选项导致每百次决策平均翻转70.4个答案，AUC从0.94降至0.23；中性名称影响较小；随机字符串回归中立控制水平；类型错误率始终为0%。

Conclusion: 模型失败源于选项名称的语义极性而非重命名操作本身，表明模型对标签语义敏感，这种敏感性会导致决策准确性大幅下降但不产生格式违规。

Abstract: Typed decision models are built for settings where model outputs are consumed directly by software. Instead of generating free-form text, they return a decision over a predefined set of options. By construction, every output conforms to the required schema. Yet this guarantee does not tell us whether the model interprets the options as intended. We study Jev and two Jev-like models with open weights by changing how option names are assigned to rubrics. Each option consists of an option name and a textual rubric that defines what the option means. We change only which option name is assigned to each rubric; the question, state, rubric wording, and set of option names remain exactly the same. On 1200 workflow decisions with task-specific rubrics, renaming the two options from 0/1 to no/yes changes 70.4 more answers per hundred (95% CI: [67.6, 73.1]) and shifts AUC from .94 to .23, revealing a systematic reversal in the decision ranking rather than simple uncertainty. The same operation has little effect with neutral option names. This pattern holds across all 4 predicates, where the effect is at least 7.4x larger than under the neutral control, and becomes stronger as the number of options increases. The effect also depends on the read-out geometry: a second model family that mean-pools over the full option span flips 4.1x less often. The hosted model exhibits the same behavior: the swap changes AUC from .8146 to .5806 and produces 24x as many answer flips as its test-retest floor. In contrast, replacing the option names with random character strings returns all model families to the neutral-control regime without reducing accuracy. The failure therefore depends on the semantic polarity of the option names rather than on the renaming operation itself. Across all conditions, the type-error rate remains 0%, even when decision accuracy degrades substantially.

</details>


### [288] [Grow the Harness, Not the Context: From Strategy-Free Scaffolds to Reusable Specialist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26760)
*Laizhen Li,Jiarui Li,Juanjuan Zhao,Kejiang Ye,Ye Li,Cheng-zhong Xu,Xitong Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Growing Harness，一种通过失败引导的训练范式，将重复的控制逻辑转化为可复用的执行代码，从而显著减少大模型调用次数并降低推理成本，同时保持甚至提升在小型模型上的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）智能体在处理相关任务流时，标准框架会在每个任务的上下文中重复要求模型重建相同的控制决策，导致资源浪费且难以适应小参数模型。旨在探索是否可以通过任务反馈将 recurring control（重复性控制）转化为可执行的代码，仅保留 LLM 用于特定语义推理。

Method: 引入 "Growing Harness" 范式：1. 从无策略脚手架开始，暴露固定的模型和工具接口，不编码任务解决控制器；2. 利用函数级执行轨迹将失败定位到有限的代码表面；3. 优化器联合修复故障窗口；4. 使用“成功优先”的保留集门控机制回滚损害先前能力的修复序列；5. 接受的编辑累积到一个共享的 harness 中，使控制结构从任务反馈中涌现。

Result: 在 BrowseComp-Plus 和 WebArena-Verified 基准测试上，跨越 4B 至 120B 参数的三种部署模型：1. 在六项基准-模型设置中的五项取得最高平均成功率，第六项仅落后最佳结果 0.7 pp；2. 相比 Tool-Calling 智能体，LLM 调用减少 76.0-91.8%，部署代理推理成本降低 74.4-98.6%；3. 在 WebArena-Verified 上，其成功率在小模型（4B）下仍保持在 44.7-45.3%，而 Tool-Calling 降至 6.7%；4. 消融实验验证了轨迹局部编辑、联合修复和基于门控的回滚均能提升最终成功率。

Conclusion: 持续的程序增长可以将重复性控制从模型上下文中移出并转化为低成本代码，从而生成可复用的专家智能体，即使在使用较小的部署模型时也能保持高效能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents often handle streams of related tasks, yet standard harnesses repeatedly ask the model to reconstruct the same control decisions inside each task's context. We study whether task feedback can instead turn recurring control into reusable executable code, while reserving LLM calls for task-specific semantic reasoning. We introduce Growing Harness, a failure-guided training paradigm that learns the agent harness itself from a strategy-free scaffold that exposes fixed model and tool interfaces but encodes no task-solving controller. Function-level execution traces localize each failure to a bounded code surface, an optimizer repairs a window of failures jointly, and a success-first held-out gate rolls back repair sequences that harm prior capability. Accepted edits accumulate in one shared harness, allowing its control structure to emerge from task feedback. Across BrowseComp-Plus and WebArena-Verified with three deployment models from 4B to 120B parameters, Growing Harness achieves the highest mean success in five of six benchmark-model settings and trails the best mean by 0.7 pp. in the sixth. Relative to a Tool-Calling agent, it reduces LLM calls by 76.0-91.8% and deployed-agent inference cost by 74.4-98.6%. On WebArena-Verified, its success remains 44.7-45.3% across model scales, whereas Tool-Calling falls to 6.7% with the 4B model. Ablations show that trace-local edits, joint repair, and gate-based rollback each improve final success. These results show that persistent program growth can move recurring control out of model context and into low-cost code, yielding reusable specialist agents that remain effective with smaller deployment models.

</details>


### [289] [SWE-Serve: Benchmarking Agentic Engineering For Production Inference Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26777)
*Jennifer Williams,Dave Farris,Jeff Farris,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SWE-Serve是一个用于评估智能体在生产环境推理工程任务中表现的基准测试。它通过53个基于SGLang实际生产变更的任务，揭示了智能体在本地完成任务与实现生产级正确性之间的巨大差距，特别是端到端（E2E）服务测试会拒绝约三分之一的局部通过补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试存在局限性：仓库级软件工程基准不针对推理领域，通用终端智能体基准包含的推理任务极少，而专门的推理基准主要关注孤立的内核生成或性能优化，缺乏对仓库规模的生产特性实现的覆盖。因此，需要一个新的基准来评估智能体在生产推理工程中的实际能力。

Method: 提出SWE-Serve基准，包含53个源自SGLang近期生产变更的仓库级任务，涵盖六大推理工程家族。任务在CPU或单GPU (H100)上执行，并通过隐藏的函数式、回归测试（包括端到端服务测试和校准的性能门限）进行评估。此外，还使用了可执行的空操作和控制组、对抗性验证器审查及闭卷执行以确保任务有效性和评估完整性。

Result: 在11个模型和31种模型-努力配置下，最佳配置的平均pass@1达到75%。在对19个具有端到端覆盖的任务进行评估时，尽管有45.9%的补丁通过了验证器测试，但排除E2E测试后得分更高（69.4%），且E2E测试拒绝了大约三分之一的局部通过补丁。这表明从局部正确性到生产正确性存在显著鸿沟。

Conclusion: SWE-Serve通过量化生产正确性差距，使该差距可直接测量。这有助于领域追踪未来智能体是否能超越仅在本地完成任务，从而真正实现生产环境的正确性。

Abstract: We introduce SWE-Serve, a benchmark for evaluating agents on production inference engineering tasks. Implementing an inference feature can require coordinating multiple changes across the serving stack, including model support, runtime execution, and public APIs. Existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of production inference engineering: repository-level software engineering benchmarks do not target inference, while general terminal-agent benchmarks include only a few inference tasks. Dedicated inference benchmarks, meanwhile, focus primarily on isolated kernel generation or performance optimization rather than repository-scale production feature implementation. SWE-Serve provides 53 repository-grounded tasks derived from recent production changes to SGLang, spanning six inference engineering families. Each task executes on either CPU or a single GPU (H100) and is evaluated with hidden functional and regression tests, including, where applicable, end-to-end (E2E) serving tests and calibrated performance gates. Executable no-op and oracle controls, adversarial verifier review, and closed-book execution support task validity and evaluation integrity. Across 11 models and 31 model-effort configurations, the best-performing configuration achieves 75% mean pass@1. SWE-Serve exposes a substantial gap between completing tasks locally and achieving production correctness. On 19 tasks with end-to-end coverage, model-serving E2E tests reject roughly one-third of patches that pass every other test (45.9% under the verifier versus 69.4% with E2E tests excluded from scoring), with pass rate increasing for each model's best-performing configuration. By making the production correctness gap directly measurable, SWE-Serve enables the field to track whether future agents move beyond completing tasks locally to achieving production correctness.

</details>


### [290] [CliffCompaction: Cost-Efficient Compaction for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26779)
*Trang Nguyen,Eulrang Cho,Bingqing Chen,Tim Dettmers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CliffCompaction是一种自动压缩技术，通过仅截断或丢弃内容（不重写）来保持上下文忠实度，在有限上下文窗口下将成本降低高达50%，同时提升Terminal-Bench和KernelBench上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决智能体在处理需数百万token复杂问题时，因上下文窗口限制而需在会话间进行压缩的需求，旨在平衡性能与计算成本。

Method: 提出CliffCompaction自动压缩技术：1) 仅对原始内容进行截断或丢弃，绝不改写；2) 每次压缩仅作用于原始内容，丢弃先前压缩输出，防止上下文漂移累积；3) 提供开源的通用API代理实现。

Result: 1) 在受限上下文中成本降低高达50%，Terminal-Bench性能提升超10个百分点且成本低于两次全上下文运行；2) Kimi K2.6配合该技术可匹敌Opus 4.7并超越Opus 4.6及GPT-5.3 Codex；3) KernelBench上CUDA内核加速比达2.23x（200步）和3.58x（400步），优于专用搜索算法。

Conclusion: CliffCompaction通过防止上下文漂移维持了百万token级会话的持续学习能力，实现了高效的时间扩展测试（test-time scaling），为通用大模型提供了低成本、高性能的上下文管理方案。

Abstract: Agents often work on complex problems that require millions of tokens of context, which necessitates compacting across sessions due to limited context windows. We develop CliffCompaction, an autocompaction technique that reduces cost by up to 50% under a bounded context while maintaining or improving performance on Terminal-Bench and achieving new levels of efficiency for test-time scaling and state-of-the-art results on KernelBench. The per-rollout savings of CliffCompaction make the performance--cost trade-off of test-time scaling more efficient, adding over 10 percentage points on Terminal-Bench for less than the cost of two full-context runs. Under parallel test-time scaling, CliffCompaction lets Kimi K2.6 match Opus 4.7, and exceed Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.3 Codex at lower cost. The key to CliffCompaction's effectiveness is that it keeps compacted information faithful by only truncating or dropping content, never rephrasing or rewriting it. We never compact a compaction---each pass operates only on original content, and prior compacted output is discarded, preventing context drift from accumulating. These properties sustain continual learning over sessions exceeding a million tokens: on KernelBench, CliffCompaction reaches CUDA kernel speedups of $2.23\times$ after 200 steps and $3.58\times$ after 400 steps, surpassing specialized search algorithms and trained agents despite being a general-purpose compaction technique. We open-source a scaffold-agnostic API-proxy implementation of CliffCompaction usable with Claude Code, Codex and other harnesses.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [291] [Spend Classification Without Leakage: An Evaluation Harness and What It Changed in a Deployed System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25502)
*Harshit Gupta*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对企业支出分析中商品代码分配缺乏公共基准的问题，构建了包含126万条标注购买记录的测试框架。研究发现传统基线在重复文本上表现良好但在新颖文本上显著下降，嵌入检索缩小了差距但微调Transformer未能完全克服；泄漏的数据分割会掩盖模型真实差异。最终在生产部署中验证了方法的有效性，并公开了该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自由文本采购行商品代码分配准确性无法检查、现有研究使用专有数据且协议未记录导致结果不可比、以及缺乏公共基准的问题。

Method: 构建了一个包含四种协议的测试框架，使用来自两个美国州政府的126万条标注购买行数据。通过对比不同数据分割策略（随机分割 vs 防泄漏分割）和不同模型（经典基线、嵌入检索、微调Transformer），评估其在重复文本和新生文本上的性能。同时推导了仅基于文本的分类器准确率上限。

Result: 1. 经典基线在重复文本准确率为56.0%，而在新生文本仅为31.9%，存在24个百分点的差距，且随分类法深度增加而扩大。
2. 嵌入检索将上述差距从23.8点缩小至19.1点；微调Transformer未能消除这一差距。
3. 在存在数据泄漏的评估中，检索与Transformer差异不明显；但在三种无泄漏协议下，Transformer领先2.3至3.6个百分点，证明泄漏分割会掩盖真实差异。
4. 仅基于文本的分类器准确率上限在不同语料库间差异巨大（79.4% vs 98.4%），表明跨数据集比较准确率无效。
5. 在生产部署的920,927条记录中，审查员接受新颖文本建议的比例为32.4%，与基准率（31.9%-34.8%）相近。

Conclusion: 现有的数据分割方式容易因数据泄漏而误导模型性能评估，无泄漏协议能更真实地反映模型差异。仅依赖文本信息的分类存在理论精度上限，且跨数据集比较需慎重。本研究建立的公共基准框架有助于推动企业支出分析领域的标准化评估，并已投入生产使用。

Abstract: Assigning a standard commodity code to a free-text purchase line underpins enterprise spend analytics, and its reported accuracy cannot be checked. Published results use proprietary data or private samples under undocumented protocols; no two compare and there is no public benchmark. We build a harness with four protocols over 1.26 million labelled purchase lines from two US state governments. Enterprises rebuy continuously, so random splits put matching item text on both sides: 60.7% and 61.2% of test rows, 54.7% and 60.6% byte for byte. On California orders the best classical baseline scores 56.0% on repeated text but 31.9% on novel text, a 24-point gap widening with taxonomy depth. Embedding retrieval shrinks the gap from 23.8 to 19.1 points; a fine-tuned transformer does not escape it. On one corpus leaky evaluation cannot separate retrieval from the transformer, while three leak-free protocols put the transformer 2.3 to 3.6 points ahead, so leaky splits hide real differences, not just flatter both. We derive an upper bound on text-only classifiers from identical text with conflicting codes: 79.4% commodity accuracy on one corpus against 98.4% on the other, so accuracy does not compare across datasets. Spend-weighting the bound reflects the amount field more than labels. In a deployment of 920,927 lines, reviewers accepted 32.4% of suggestions on the novel-text population; 29.7-35.2% brackets benchmark rates near 31.9% and 34.8%. We release the harness in production use.

</details>


### [292] [AkasicMEM: Governed Enterprise Memory for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25563)
*Jeongmin Bae,Yongjae Kim,Kyoung Hur,Donghyoung Han,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出AkasicMEM，一种基于GraphAI AkasicDB的受控企业记忆系统，通过授权连续性解决源-记忆集成中的信息泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法单独处理源-记忆集成、记忆治理和授权连续性，未将其作为贯穿记忆生命周期的核心设计目标，导致在信息从企业源持久化到记忆并反复重用时，可能绕过源端限制造成信息泄露。

Method: 定义“受控企业记忆”（Governed Enterprise Memory），实现机制包括：通过传递性谱系（transitive lineage）、记忆形成时的策略组合、检索时的策略重新评估来实现授权连续性；底层构建于统一向量-图-关系数据库AkasicDB之上，以联合优化存储与执行。

Result: 成功实现了AkasicMEM原型，验证了其在保持组织策略约束的同时，支持企业智能体间知识的跨任务复用与安全治理。

Conclusion: 将源-记忆集成、记忆治理与授权连续性结合为企业记忆设计的核心要素，可有效防止因策略失效导致的信息泄露，为构建安全、可持续的企业级Agent记忆系统提供新范式。

Abstract: Agent memory enables enterprise agents to retain knowledge acquired during work and reuse it across tasks and agents, turning execution experience into persistent organizational knowledge. Realizing this potential requires both source--memory integration, through which enterprise sources and accumulated memory can be utilized together, and memory governance, through which shared memory remains subject to organizational policies throughout its lifecycle. These requirements interact when information from enterprise sources persists in memory. As this information is repeatedly derived and reused under changing principals and policies, source restrictions may be bypassed, resulting in information leakage. Preventing such leakage requires authorization continuity, under which source restrictions remain effective throughout source-to-memory and memory-to-memory derivation and reuse. Existing approaches address these concerns individually, but do not treat source--memory integration, memory governance, and authorization continuity as combined core design targets across the memory lifecycle. We define Governed Enterprise Memory as agent memory designed around this combined scope and present AkasicMEM as its realization. AkasicMEM realizes authorization continuity through transitive lineage, policy composition during memory formation, and policy re-evaluation during retrieval. It is built on GraphAI's AkasicDB, a unified vector--graph--relational database whose storage and execution substrate enables the underlying operations of these mechanisms to be jointly optimized and executed.

</details>


### [293] [Generating Query Context for Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26200)
*Alekh Jindal,Jyoti Pandey,Christina Pavlopoulou,Ronith PR,Sharath Prakash,Shi Qiao,Shivani Tripathi,Wangda Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动为关系数据库生成查询上下文（问题-数据模型对）的方法，以解决自然语言接口查询SQL时的冷启动和维护难题。该方法通过定义查询流、遍历生成数据模型并采样实例化值，已在50多个真实数据库中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI模型在通过自然语言接口查询关系数据库时，需要配对的问题和数据模型片段作为上下文才能生成准确的SQL。目前这一过程依赖人工，耗时且需SQL和模式专业知识，导致新数据库面临“冷启动”问题，且随模式演变需持续维护。

Method: 1. 定义捕获常见数据检索和分析问题模式的“查询流”；2. 系统性地遍历这些查询流以生成数据模型；3. 使用加权策略从数据库中采样具体值进行实例化，以最大化多样性和覆盖率。

Result: 成功生成了用于指导自然语言接口准确查询关系数据库的“问题-数据模型”对。该方案已在连接至Tursio系统的50多个真实世界数据库上部署并验证。

Conclusion: 自动化生成查询上下文的方法能有效降低自然语言查询接口的构建和维护成本，提升SQL生成的准确性，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Relational databases are the systems of record for business applications, and there is growing demand to query them through natural language interfaces. A key challenge is that AI models need appropriate query context, i.e., sample questions paired with their corresponding data-model fragments, to generate accurate SQL. Today, creating this context is a manual, time-consuming process that requires expertise in both SQL and the database schema, leading to a cold start problem for new databases and an ongoing maintenance burden as schemas and query patterns evolve. We present an automated approach for generating query context for relational databases. Our method defines query flows that capture common patterns of data retrieval and analysis questions, systematically generates data models by traversing these flows, and instantiates them with specific values sampled from the database using weighted strategies that maximize diversity and coverage. The resulting question--data-model pairs can be used to guide natural language interfaces in accurately querying relational databases. We report on our experience deploying this approach across more than 50 real-world databases connected to Tursio.

</details>


### [294] [Proof-of-Retention: A Framework for Auditable Cross-Organization Data Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26654)
*Kyle MacMillan,Sanjay Krishnan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出Proof-of-Retention框架，通过结合数据库查询见证生成与密码学证据协议，实现可审计的数据共享保留证明，解决监管合规中的隐私保护与验证难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据共享加速了AI发展，但违反隐私规范；现有法规难以准确评估合规性，因数据溯源信息（metadata）很少被保留，且单方数据库变更易导致溯源信息失效。

Method: 构建Proof-of-Retention交互式协议，要求参与方保留数据交换的特定信息子集；利用查询见证生成技术与密码学所有权证明协议，使审计员能验证信息保留情况，无需全量数据复制或侵入式监控。

Result: 形式化验证了协议的安全性及性能属性，提供了基于AWS基础设施的参考实现，实现了高效、隐私保护且实用的数据共享审计机制。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了数据共享合规性审计的技术空白，在保障隐私的同时提供了可验证的数据保留保证，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of AI across industries for (e.g.) fine-tuning and analytics has accelerated the need for high-quality data. To satisfy this demand, public and private entities will buy and sell data with other organizations. But such data sharing can and does violate privacy norms and laws. EU and American lawmakers have endeavored to control data sharing, restricting what data may be shared with whom, and under what circumstances. Unfortunately, accurately assessing compliance with new regulatory regimes remains a challenge, as data provenance, that is, metadata about data sharing, is rarely preserved. And even when provenance information is retained, unilateral changes to one party's database can render the provenance stale.
  To fill this gap, we present Proof-of-Retention a novel framework and interactive protocol that enforces auditable data sharing. Our framework requires each party involved in data sharing to retain a subset of information associated with each data exchange, and provides a mechanism for auditors to verify that the parties have indeed retained that information. Our approach combines techniques from database systems, including query witness generation, with cryptographic proof-of-possession protocols to provide retention guarantees. The framework enables efficient auditing that does not require full data replication or intrusive monitoring, thereby preserving privacy and remaining practical. We formalize the protocol, analyze its security and performance properties, and provide a reference implementation built on AWS infrastructure.

</details>


### [295] [Databases with Missing Values that are Governed by Missingness Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26692)
*Leopoldo Bertossi,Farouk Toumani*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出基于贝叶斯网络（缺失图）为含缺失值的关系数据库赋予语义，构建块独立概率数据库，并确定两个最优可能世界类以支持查询分析，同时提供了计算复杂性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决含有缺失值（MVs）的关系数据库的语义定义问题，特别是区别于传统NULL值处理机制，旨在结合缺失机制模型与观测数据来量化不确定性及统计合理性。

Method: 将缺失机制建模为涉及数据库属性的贝叶斯网络（称为缺失图MG），结合MG与观测数据库构建块独立概率数据库，识别用于查询分析的两种最优可能世界类，并分析其计算复杂度。

Result: 成功构建了基于缺失图的块独立概率数据库；确定了捕获概率不确定性和隐式插补统计合理性的两个最优可能世界类；获得了部分最优类的可计算性结果以及该方法整体计算可行性的复杂性特征。

Conclusion: 该框架通过缺失图有效处理数据库中的缺失值，为查询分析提供了理论依据和计算可行性保障，显著区别于传统的NULL值处理方法。

Abstract: We address the problems of giving a semantics to a relational database (RDB) that has missing values (MVs). The causes for the latter are governed by a Missingness Mechanism that is modelled as a Bayesian Network (BN) that involves the DB attributes as variables. The BN is called a Missingness Graph (MG). Our approach considerable departs from the treatment of RDBs with NULL (values). The combination of the MG and the observed DB allows us to build a block-independent probabilistic DB. We identify two optimal classes of its possible worlds on which QA can be performed. Those classes jointly capture probabilistic uncertainty and statistical plausibility of the implicit imputation of MVs. We obtain tractability results for the computation of some optimal classes; and we also obtain complexity results that characterize the computational feasibility of our approach.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [GroundedGEO: Auditing the Evidence Gap in Generative Search Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25189)
*Yihan Xia,Huiling Fan,Kangrong Zhong,Taotao Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究揭示了生成式搜索系统中仅依赖文本的排序和防御无法区分真实内容与伪造细节，存在“可识别性缺口”。通过构建证据配对基准和提出GroundedGEO重排器，研究发现缺乏支持的丰富变体在排名上显著获益，而自动裁判均未能达到可靠性标准，指出了标签质量和包覆盖度两大限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式搜索系统用于对产品和服务进行排名以支持重大决策，发布者可以低成本使候选文本看起来相关。然而，证据状态并非单纯的文本属性，而是主张与证据之间的关系。现有的纯文本排序器和防御措施无法区分诚实的详细内容和伪造的细节，导致存在可识别性缺口（identifiability gap），需要对此进行审计和评估。

Method: 1. 构建了包含50个电商查询、1,950个案例的证据配对基准（evidence-paired benchmark）。
2. 提出了基于声明级别的重排器 GroundedGEO，对缺乏供应数据包支持的查询相关声明进行惩罚。
3. 使用匹配丰富变体控制格式和数量；使用数据包孪生体（packet twins）在固定文本处添加或移除证明（attestations）。
4. 在冻结的列表式排序器（Qwen2.5-7B）、点式评分器以及针对370个声明的人类黄金标准上进行测试，评估自动裁判的可靠性。

Result: 1. 在 Qwen2.5-7B 上，缺乏支持的丰富变体相比干净候选项显示出显著的归一化排名增益（+0.065 至 +0.092），而受支持和中性控制组则无此效果；该效应具有模型依赖性（在 MiMo-v2.5 上边缘显著，在 GLM-5.3-Flash 上不存在）。
2. 在冻结的点式评分器中，使用oracle证据标签可将不支持丰富的top-3率从0.65降至0.43，将洗白率从0.61降至0.39，且零误报抑制；数据包孪生体在不改变文本的情况下恢复了原始比率。
3. 所有测试的自动裁判均未通过预注册的可靠性门槛，尽管最佳本地裁判保留了79-100%的oracle抑制能力且无测量到的误报抑制。
4. 剥离证明覆盖范围使误报抑制增加了0.307。

Conclusion: 诊断效应确定了证据通道的两个限制：标签质量和数据包覆盖度。本研究并未验证一种有效的自动防御机制，且关于不利人类黄金标准臂的解释仍有待裁决。这表明当前自动方法难以有效解决生成式内容中的证据真实性问题。

Abstract: Generative search systems rank products and services for consequential decisions, and publishers can cheaply make candidate text look relevant. Yet evidence status is not a text property but a claim-evidence relation: text-only rankers and defenses cannot separate honest detailed content from fabricated detail, creating an identifiability gap. We audit this gap with an evidence-paired benchmark (50 e-commerce queries, 1,950 cases) and a claim-level reranker, GroundedGEO, that penalizes query-relevant claims lacking support in a supplied packet. Matched rich variants control format and volume; packet twins add attestations at fixed text, while thinned packets withdraw them. On the frozen listwise ranker Qwen2.5-7B, unsupported-rich variants show significant normalized rank gain over clean candidates (+0.065 to +0.092 across claim profiles, Holm-corrected), while supported and neutral controls do not; the effect is model-dependent (marginal on MiMo-v2.5, absent on GLM-5.3-Flash). On a frozen pointwise scorer, oracle evidence labels cut the unsupported-rich top-3 rate from 0.65 to 0.43 (laundering from 0.61 to 0.39) at lambda=40 with zero false suppression; packet twins restore the original rates without changing text. Against a 370-claim human gold, all tested automatic judges fail the preregistered reliability gate, although the best local judge retains 79-100% of oracle suppression with zero measured false suppression on protected arms. Separately, stripping attestation coverage increases false suppression by 0.307. These diagnostic effects identify two limits on the evidence channel: label quality and packet coverage. They do not validate an automatic defense, and interpretation of the adverse human-gold arm remains pending adjudication.

</details>


### [297] [ReFilter: Bridging Embeddings and LLM Filtering for Similar Mobile App Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25306)
*Buthayna AlMulla,Maram Assi,Safwat Hassan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ReFilter框架，结合嵌入检索与大语言模型过滤，解决移动应用功能相似性检索中精度低的问题，实现90%的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于嵌入的检索方法仅能捕捉语义相似性，无法识别功能相似的App；且因计算成本高，此前无工作尝试利用大语言模型（LLM）进行过滤以提升精度。

Method: 提出混合框架ReFilter：首先使用嵌入技术检索语义相关的候选App，随后应用基于LLM的上下文过滤机制，以高精度识别真正功能相似的App。

Result: 该设计平衡了效率与准确性，在检索相似App的任务中达到了90%的F1分数。

Conclusion: ReFilter提高了App替代方案的关联性，支持更准确的App比较、生态系统理解、竞品分析及推荐服务。

Abstract: Retrieving similar mobile applications (apps) is essential for researchers, developers, and end-users. Researchers use similarity detection to study app ecosystems and trends, developers for competitor analysis, and end-users for focused app recommendations. Existing approaches rely on embedding-based retrieval, which captures semantic similarity but fails to identify functionally similar apps. To our knowledge, no prior work has applied large language model (LLM)-based filtering to this task, due to the high computational cost of evaluating large numbers of app pairs. To address this gap, we propose ReFilter, a hybrid framework that first Retrieves semantically related candidate apps using embeddings and then applies LLM-based contextual Filtering to identify true functionally similar apps with higher precision. This design balances efficiency and accuracy, achieving an F1-score of 90% for retrieving similar apps. By improving the relevance of app alternatives, ReFilter enables more accurate app comparisons and supports improved ecosystem understanding, competitor analysis, and recommendations.

</details>


### [298] [Discovery-Driven Integration of Disjoint Tables via Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26658)
*Md Ataur Rahman,Dimitris Sacharidis,Oscar Romero,Sergi Nadal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LOKI模型，通过文本中介的跨注意力架构解决异构数据湖中细粒度行级连接发现难题，实现高精度、低成本的集成表生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据湖整合异构数据集时，语义相关表常缺乏显式属性以进行连接。现有方法多依赖粗粒度的列-文本关联或预设的行-文本链接，难以发现连接不同表中具体行的细粒度关系。

Method: 形式化为“文本中介的连接路径发现”任务，提出LOKI（Latent-space Optimization for Knowledge Integration）模型。该模型采用水平双向跨注意力架构，学习表格行和句子的上下文表示，并通过全局表-文本对比目标在无局部监督下涌现细粒度行-句子关联，将隐式关联转化为显式的、可解释的连接路径。

Result: 在真实世界基准测试中，LOKI持续优于最先进的方法，生成的类型化集成表达到0.982的宏观类型对精度，且LLM API成本比直接提示法低多达40倍。

Conclusion: LOKI能有效从非结构化文本中发现细粒度连接关系，构建带句子级溯源的类型化集成表，为异构数据整合提供了高效、精准且经济的新范式。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous datasets within data lakes is a critical challenge, particularly for semantically related tables that lack the explicit attributes needed to be joined. We study Discovery-Driven Integration, where the relevant sources and their missing relational structure must be discovered before integration. In this setting, unstructured text provides the evidence that connects otherwise disjoint tables. The fundamental challenge is to discover the relationships at a fine-grained level that connect individual rows from different tables through specific sentences. We formalize this task as Text-Mediated Join Path Discovery and propose a horizontal bidirectional cross-attention architecture called LOKI Latent-space Optimization for Knowledge Integration) that learns contextualized representations of table rows and sentences. Through a global table-text contrastive objective, fine-grained row-sentence associations emerge without explicit local supervision. Existing multi-modal discovery methods largely retrieve coarse-grained column-text associations, whereas integration systems assume supplied row-text links, schemas, or queries. LOKI instead transforms these implicit associations into explicit, interpretable join paths, organizes them into relation-consistent groups, and materializes them as typed integrated tables with sentence-level provenance. Comprehensive evaluations on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that LOKI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-modal data discovery approaches, and materializes typed integrated tables with 0.982 macro typed-pair precision while being up to 40 times cheaper in LLM API cost than direct prompting.

</details>


### [299] [Robust Fusion of Semantic and Behavioural Signals for LLM Reranking in Personalised Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25825)
*Aleksandr V. Petrov,Nathan Stein,Erik Lybecker,Emma Schüldt,Daniel Lazarovski,Hugues Bouchard,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对大模型个性化搜索中注入历史行为统计（QSS）导致的“捷径学习”和鲁棒性下降问题，提出确定性双样本特征丢弃训练方法。该方法通过交替呈现含/不含QSS的样本进行训练，在保持排名质量提升的同时，显著增强了模型在无历史数据场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based cross-encoders在个性化搜索中虽能提供单一重排序接口，但直接注入预测性行为统计（如历史成功信号）易导致模型过度依赖历史信号，产生“捷径学习”，从而损害对语义和用户上下文模式的泛化能力，特别是在稀疏或未见过的搜索场景中表现不佳。

Method: 在大规模音频流媒体平台的个性化搜索系统中，研究基于Query Slice Stats (QSS) 的行为特征。为解决Naive QSS注入导致的鲁棒性降低问题，提出**确定性双样本特征丢弃训练（deterministic dual-sample feature-dropout training）**。具体而言，对每个示例同时提供两次输入：一次包含QSS，一次不包含QSS，以此平衡利用强行为统计与保持无数据时的鲁棒性。

Result: 1. **离线评估**：QSS注入使排名质量提升13.3%；双样本训练在保留该增益的同时，使QSS移除情况下的性能比Naive训练提升4.0%。
2. **在线测试**：两种QSS感知变体均使搜索成功率提高约2%。
3. **对比分析**：聚合测试中双样本与仅特征训练无显著差异，但在冷启动比较中与离线结果方向一致，验证了其在缺乏行为数据时的有效性。

Conclusion: 成对的特征存在与缺失训练能够有效缓解利用强行为统计与保持无数据时鲁棒性之间的张力，为个性化搜索系统提供了一种兼顾精度与泛化能力的训练范式。

Abstract: Personalised search must satisfy query intent while incorporating user context and historical interactions. LLM-based cross-encoders provide a single reranking interface, but injecting predictive behavioural statistics into their prompts can encourage shortcut learning: reliance on historical signals at the expense of semantic and user-context patterns that generalise to sparse or unseen searches.
  We study this problem in the personalised search system of a large-scale audio streaming platform using Query Slice Stats (QSS), an interaction-derived behavioural feature summarising historical success for query-candidate pairs. Naive QSS injection improves ranking when the feature is available but reduces robustness when it is removed. We address this with deterministic dual-sample feature-dropout training, which presents each example once with QSS included and once with QSS removed.
  Offline, QSS injection improves ranking quality by 13.3% when available. Dual-sample training preserves these gains while improving performance under QSS-removed evaluation by 4.0% relative to naive QSS training. In a live online test, both QSS-aware variants improve search success by roughly 2%. The aggregate test does not distinguish dual-sample from features-only training; the cold-start comparison is directionally consistent with the offline results.
  Paired feature-present and feature-removed training can therefore reduce the tension between exploiting strong behavioural statistics and remaining robust when they are unavailable.

</details>


### [300] [Knowledge-as-Skill: A Structural Design for Autonomous Knowledge-Base Use by LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25991)
*Jiangxu Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出“知识即技能”（Knowledge-as-Skill）框架，通过分层结构使知识库对智能体可发现、可导航且自描述，解决传统RAG中检索决策瓶颈问题。在WixQA基准测试中，事实性和上下文召回率显著优于基线，但忠实度和精度略低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索增强生成（RAG）的“检索-拼接-生成”流程限制了模型自主性，且传统知识库以匿名文本块形式存在，缺乏关于范围、目的、来源或关系的元数据，导致智能体难以有效决定何时检索、检索什么以及何时停止。

Method: 提出Knowledge-as-Skill组织方案，包含三层：以SKILL.md为中心的发现层、每目录一个index.md的导航层、以及带有YAML前置元数据（主题、类型、来源、生命周期）的知识层。该设计遵循开放知识格式（OKF）和技能协议，并提供将异构文件转换为该结构的管道，且不修改智能体框架本身。

Result: 在WixQA企业客服基准测试中，该方法获得0.889的事实性（Factuality）和0.816的上下文召回率（Context Recall），优于基线的0.767和0.708。但在忠实度（Faithfulness）、上下文精确度（Context Precision）及交互轮次方面表现略差。

Conclusion: Knowledge-as-Skill通过结构化元数据增强了知识库的可发现性和导航性，提升了关键指标如事实性和召回率。尽管存在其他指标下降的情况，但结果证明了该方向的有效性，需进一步控制变量进行对比研究。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) gives large language models (LLMs) access to external knowledge, but its conventional retrieve-concatenate-generate pipeline makes retrieval decisions on behalf of the model. As tool use and agent loops become more reliable, an agent can decide whether to retrieve, what to inspect, and when to stop. This shift exposes a new bottleneck: the agent may not know what a knowledge base contains. Traditional knowledge bases expose documents as anonymous text chunks with limited information about scope, purpose, provenance, or relations.
  We propose Knowledge-as-Skill, an organization scheme that makes a knowledge base discoverable, navigable, and self-descriptive. It has three layers: a discovery layer centered on SKILL.md; a navigation layer with one index.md per directory; and a knowledge layer containing documents with YAML frontmatter for topic, type, provenance, and lifecycle. The design follows the Open Knowledge Format (OKF) and the Skill protocol without modifying the agent framework. We also provide knowledge-as-skill, a pipeline for converting heterogeneous collections of PDFs, Word files, web exports, and notes into this structure.
  In a preliminary evaluation on the WixQA enterprise customer-support benchmark, our setup obtains 0.889 Factuality and 0.816 Context Recall, compared with reported Corpus2Skill values of 0.767 and 0.708. It obtains slightly lower Faithfulness, lower Context Precision, and more interaction turns. Because the models, prompts, and knowledge-package construction differ, these results are directional cross-work evidence rather than a controlled comparison.

</details>


### [301] [TailSpec-EASE: Knowledge-Graph-Regularized Linear Recommendation for Web Long-Tail Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26143)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TailSpec-EASE，一种通过注入关系感知谱KG先验到局部闭式重建目标中的轻量级推荐系统，以解决流行物品过度服务及长尾物品被忽视的问题，在精度、长尾性能和训练成本间取得优异平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图神经网络的KG感知推荐器计算复杂，而强大的浅层线性模型（如EASE）通常忽略侧信息或在闭式版本中不可行；同时Web平台推荐系统易导致热门物品过载并忽视长尾物品。

Method: 引入TailSpec-EASE算法，将关系感知的谱知识图谱（KG）先验注入到局部闭式重建目标中。该先验强度根据物品流行度自适应调整，为长尾物品提供更强的语义指导。

Result: 在四个公共基准测试中，相比无KG信息的对应模型，NDCG@20最高提升24%；长尾指标改进具有统计显著性；在Amazon-book实验中，CPU训练仅需37秒（远低于GPU KGAT的2584秒和CPU LightGCN的15800秒），且精度更高；适用于全局闭式模型内存溢出的大规模目录场景。

Conclusion: TailSpec-EASE成功实现了高精度、优异的长尾性能与极低训练成本的平衡，证明了结合KG先验的轻量级局部闭式模型在推荐系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Recommender systems on Web platforms tend to over-serve popular items and neglect the long tail. Item-side knowledge graphs (KGs), often available as linked data or RDF-style Web resources, can help by connecting sparse items through shared semantic attributes. Many competitive KG-aware recommenders rely on graph neural architectures, whereas strong shallow linear models such as EASE-R typically ignore side information and may become infeasible in their global closed-form version. We introduce TailSpec-EASE, a lightweight recommender that injects a relation-aware spectral KG prior into a local closed-form reconstruction objective. The prior strength adapts to item popularity, giving stronger semantic guidance to long-tail items. Across four public benchmarks and a broad set of classical, linear, graph-CF, KG-aware neural, and score-level KG baselines, TailSpec-EASE attains a favorable trade-off between overall accuracy, long-tail performance, and training cost. It improves NDCG@20 by up to 24% over its counterpart without KG information. All tail-metric improvements over the no-KG counterpart are statistically significant under a paired bootstrap, and overall NDCG improves significantly on three of the four datasets. In a representative Amazon-book timing study, TailSpec-EASE trains in 37 seconds on CPU, compared with 2,584 seconds for a GPU-trained KGAT run and 15,800 seconds for CPU LightGCN, while attaining higher NDCG@20 and Tail Recall@20 on that dataset. It also remains feasible on catalogs where the global closed-form model runs out of memory.

</details>


### [302] [When Concealed Links Cannot Be Recovered: A Structural Identifiability Bound and Evaluation Pitfalls in Offshore Leak Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26171)
*Joseph Bingham*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究证明，仅凭泄露数据中的公开部分，无法有效通过链接预测恢复离岸实体背后的受益所有权关系。隔离节点的隐藏边无法被识别，整体恢复率受限于节点的结构不可区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对巴拿马文件等离岸实体泄露事件，探讨能否从公开部分通过链接预测技术还原其旨在隐藏的受益所有权关系。

Method: 提出无分布可识别性界限（distribution-free identifiability bound），结合Weisfeiler-Leman颜色类分析结构不可区分性，并在ICIJ离岸泄露全图（81.4万实体）上进行实证评估，同时指出五种评估陷阱。

Result: 对于任何尊重图同构的恢复规则，孤立节点的隐藏边无法高于随机水平被恢复；恢复上限由节点的结构不可区分性类别大小决定，而非仅仅是节点度。在五个泄露数据集中均观察到相同的性能下限，且融合外部数据的效果有限。

Conclusion: 试图直接恢复隐藏的主要受益人几乎不可行，研究建议将工作重心转向揭示隐瞒机制本身，并警告现有评估方法存在误导性陷阱。

Abstract: Leaks such as the Panama and Paradise Papers expose large networks of offshore entities, and they invite an obvious question for network learning. Can the relations these structures are built to hide---above all, who beneficially owns what---be recovered from the public part of the leak by link prediction? We argue that the answer is mostly no, and that the analyses which suggest otherwise are measuring the wrong thing. Our main result is a distribution-free identifiability bound. For any recovery rule that respects graph isomorphism, and that covers every topological link-prediction score together with every message-passing graph neural network, a concealed endpoint left isolated in the observed graph is interchangeable with its structural twins, so its hidden edge cannot be recovered above chance. Isolation is only the sharpest case. In general the ceiling on recovery is set by the size of a node's structural-indistinguishability class, for which the Weisfeiler--Leman colour class is a computable stand-in, and node degree is at best a loose proxy. On the full ICIJ Offshore Leaks graph (814K entities and 84K labelled beneficial-owner edges) a classifier-free, degree-controlled probe reproduces an exact $0.5$ floor for isolated owners, who make up $20.6\%$ of all owners, and a trained graph neural network lands on the same floor. Recovery climbs only as structural distinctiveness grows, and the floor reappears in every one of the five leaks. Along the way we document five evaluation traps. Each one makes a bound that cannot be beaten look beaten, and we give a short rule that avoids them. The practical upshot is to redirect effort from the hidden principal, which is close to unrecoverable, toward the machinery of concealment, and to spell out why fusing external data helps far less than one would hope.

</details>


### [303] [A Semantic Approach to the Academic Publishing Network: Document Vector Representations and Hybrid Structural-Semantic Fusion over OpenAlex Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26218)
*Robert Šamárek,Radek Martinek*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结构-语义融合方法，结合SPECTER2向量嵌入（语义）和文献耦合（结构），通过可调权重alpha在推荐任务中灵活整合两种信号，优于单一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有学术出版网络分析仅关注拓扑结构而忽略内容语义的问题，旨在通过融合结构与语义信息提升分析深度。

Method: 使用SPECTER2生成引用感知的文档向量嵌入并存储于OpenAlex ID键值的向量数据库中；定义模块化晚期融合函数，结合余弦相似度（语义）和文献耦合（结构），引入可调参数alpha根据具体任务优化权重。

Result: 在VSB - 捷克奥斯特拉瓦理工大学语料库上验证：SPECTER2嵌入比TF-IDF基线更符合专家分类体系；在推荐场景中，结构、语义及混合信号在不同数据条件下表现各异，混合融合并非总是更优，而是作为引导互补信号的机制。

Conclusion: 结构-语义融合是一种可调节的显式机制，能根据任务需求有效整合互补信号；该方法已开源为apnet库的扩展，提供可复现的工作流程。

Abstract: Structural graph analysis of the academic publishing network captures the topological relationships between entities but does not see the content of works. Building on our structural approach, this work complements it with a semantic layer and a parameterized structural-semantic fusion. We represent scientific documents by citation-informed vector embeddings (SPECTER2) and store them in an embedded vector database keyed by the stable OpenAlex ID, so that they connect directly to the graph layer. We define a modular late-fusion function that combines semantic similarity (cosine of embeddings) and structural similarity (bibliographic coupling) with a tunable weight alpha whose value is chosen according to the specific task. On the corpus of VSB - Technical University of Ostrava we show two things: citation-informed embeddings agree with the expert OpenAlex topical taxonomy better than a TF-IDF baseline, and in a recommendation use case the structural, semantic, and combined signals carry information in different regimes depending on the available data. Hybrid fusion here is not a universally better method but an explicit mechanism for steering complementary signals according to the task. We release the whole approach as an open-source extension of the apnet library with a reproducible workflow.

</details>


### [304] [ABAI at COLIEE 2026 Task 1: Multi-Stage Retrieval with GraphRAG-Enhanced Meta-Learning, and a Post-Hoc Study of the Cross-Validation-to-Test Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26237)
*Minhan Cho,Soyoung Park,Daejin Choi,Jinyoung Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ABAI在COLIEE 2026案例法检索任务中的提交方案及性能分析。尽管采用了包含BM25、神经重排序、图注意力网络及LightGBM元学习器的四阶段流水线，模型在官方测试集上F1分数仅为0.177（交叉验证为0.311）。研究发现主要瓶颈在于召回率上限、时间分布偏移和阈值校准错误，而非数据泄露或分布差异。通过优化BM25长度归一化、引入事件三元组视图及全内容密集融合等措施，可有效提升召回率并改善最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决COLIEE 2026 Task 1中因隐去引用段落而导致的 lexical overlap（词汇重叠）缺失问题，旨在提升案例法检索的准确性，并深入分析系统性能低于预期的根本原因。

Method: 构建了一个四阶段独立训练的检索流水线：1) 基于引用上下文窗口的多视图BM25结合倒数秩融合；2) 神经重排序；3) 基于实体社区和图注意力网络的图特征提取；4) 基于34个特征的LightGBM元学习器。随后进行了受控实验以评估召回率、时间分布和阈值校准的影响，并测试了多种改进策略。

Result: 最佳运行结果在官方测试集上达到F1=0.177。误差分解显示，未命中的候选项一半从未被检索到，另一半虽被检索但排名低于截止线。实验证明阈值迁移成本极低（仅0.007 F1），且测试查询与训练查询在嵌入空间中距离无显著差异。改进措施中，BM25长度归一化调整、事件三元组视图和全内容密集融合使Top-200召回率提升3-7个百分点，去除自身引用泄露后图特征增加0.014 F1；而每查询截止规则、零样本重排序器替换和日期过滤器无效。此外，发现了四个评估伪影，修正协议后反转了部分结果。

Conclusion: COLIEE 2026案例法检索性能的主要限制因素是检索系统的召回能力不足及特征工程细节，而非数据分布漂移或评估泄露问题。通过细化BM25参数、增强语义表示（如事件三元组和密集融合）以及严格消除引用泄露，可以显著提升检索效果。同时，研究强调了评估协议严谨性的重要性，指出了先前评估中存在的若干伪影。

Abstract: We present the ABAI submission to COLIEE 2026 Task 1, case law retrieval, together with a controlled study of why it underperformed. The task suppresses the cited passages themselves, which removes much of the lexical overlap a retriever would rely on. Our pipeline answers this with four independently trained stages: multi-view BM25 over citation-context windows with reciprocal rank fusion, neural reranking, graph-based features from entity communities and a graph attention network, and a LightGBM meta-learner over 34 features. Our best run reached F1=0.177 on the official test set, against a cross-validated 0.311, and we attributed that gap to a recall ceiling, temporal distribution shift, and threshold miscalibration. We then tested all three. Under leakage-free protocols threshold transfer costs 0.007 F1, decision quality is flat across chronological quartiles, and the official test queries are not measurably farther from the training manifold than training queries are from each other, in two independent embedding spaces. Decomposing the misses instead splits them exactly evenly between candidates never retrieved and candidates retrieved but ranked below the cut. Measuring the remedies for each half, BM25 length-normalisation tuning, an event-triple view, and full-content dense fusion lift top-200 recall by three to seven points, and citation-graph features add 0.014 F1 over eight seeds once own-citation leakage is removed, while per-query cutoff rules, a zero-shot reranker swap, and a date filter do not help. We also document four evaluation artifacts, each of which reversed a result once the protocol was corrected.

</details>


### [305] [Which Reranking Conclusions Survive the Answer Interface? A Prospective Finite-Orbit Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26250)
*Wenzhang Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究评估了检索增强生成（RAG）中不同“读者”接口对BM25与BGE-v2-m3排序效果比较的影响，发现虽然存在一定程度的选择分歧，但未证实界面变化导致排序结论的根本性逆转，强调需分离策略评估与界面稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着重排序器（Rerankers）越来越多地通过下游语言模型的回答进行评估，引发了一个关键的检索测量问题：如果仅改变读者的答案接口，关于BM25与BGE-v2-m3的相对优劣结论是否依然成立？旨在验证检索评估结果是否受具体交互界面的影响。

Method: 使用RAGuard和FEVER数据集，结合四种不同的“读者”模型进行前瞻性审计。保持检索策略、证据、声明和上下文深度固定，构建八种任务等效但语义-标签绑定、词汇表及选项顺序不同的接口变体，统计各设置下检索策略效应的估计值、排序及选择价值。

Result: 在六个确认性设置中，未发现超过0.015显著性阈值的界面变异，也未出现BM25与BGE排序的确定性逆转。尽管单一环境中选择分歧达33.5%，但在八个环境均未确立预设的材料外推值差异。五个设置因不确定性过高，未能满足高阶等价条件。

Conclusion: 结果不支持广泛的复制不稳定性，但也未证明普遍不变性。建议检索评估应将策略层面、界面稳定性、策略排序和选择价值分开考量；在稳定性未验证时，应对枚举接口进行统一平均并明确变异界限，以避免偏袒特定界面。

Abstract: Rerankers are increasingly evaluated through downstream language-model answers. This raises a retrieval-measurement question: if only the reader's answer interface changes, should we reach the same conclusion about BM25 versus BGE-v2-m3? We prospectively audit their claim-paired effect on RAGuard and FEVER with four readers. Retrieval policies, evidence, claims, and context depth remain fixed while semantic-to-label binding, A/B versus X/Y vocabulary, and option order form eight task-equivalent interfaces. We ask whether the estimated retrieval-policy effect, its ordering, or selection value changes. None of the six confirmatory settings showed statistically certified interface variation above the prespecified 0.015 materiality threshold, and none showed a certified reversal of the BM25-BGE ordering. Selector disagreement reaches 33.5% in one environment, yet none of eight environments establishes the prespecified material held-out value difference. These results do not support broad replicated instability, but they do not prove universal invariance: five settings remain too uncertain to satisfy the prespecified higher-order equivalence condition. They show why retrieval evaluations should separate policy level, interface stability, policy ordering, and selection value. When stability is unverified, a uniform average over the enumerated interfaces with explicit variation bounds avoids privileging one interface.

</details>


### [306] [When Does Permutation Instability Generalize? Independent-View Validation for Listwise LLM Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26251)
*Wenzhang Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究揭示了在评估语言模型重排器的有限视图不稳定性时，验证统计量若复用探针视角会导致“部分-整体”关联偏差。结论强调，为了准确预测未见排列的预测信息，验证目标必须与探针完全正交（observation-disjoint），否则会产生误导性的高相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的有限不稳定性诊断指标常被用于激励额外的采样或聚合计算，但这些指标若复用探针视角（probe views），可能无法隔离出关于未见排列的真实预测信息，而是受到共享测量带来的经典部分-整体关联（classical part-whole association）的影响。

Method: 推导了精确的有限视图分解，并在两个7B模型家族和两个推荐数据集上进行了前瞻性比较。实验设置了零、一、两个复用视图以及完全正交的四个视图目标，包括受控列表的离线分析和未触碰检索器列表的目标自由复制。

Result: 1. 完全正交视图下的相关性微弱且异质（-0.061至0.281），而复用两个探针视图导致高相关性（0.600至0.718）。2. 在未触碰列表中，重叠效应始终为正。3. 探针能预测聚合移动幅度，但不能预测稳定的符号目标收益。4. 在12个固定分数探针路由点中，有7个在测量成本下被严格支配。

Conclusion: 当估计目标是关于未见扰动行为的预测信息时，验证目标必须与探针观察正交以隔离该信息；符号效用和成本敏感决策则是独立的问题。简单的增加探针视图数量并不总能提高正交可靠性。

Abstract: Listwise language-model rerankers often disagree across equivalent candidate permutations. Finite instability diagnostics are therefore used to motivate additional sampling, aggregation, or selective computation. But an association with a validation statistic that reuses the probe views need not isolate predictive information about unseen permutations. Shared measurements can induce classical part-whole association. We study how this affects claims that a finite-view instability score predicts unseen permutations. We derive the exact finite-view decomposition and prospectively compare zero, one, and two reused views, including a fully disjoint four-view target.
  The study covers two pinned 7B model families and two recommendation datasets, with controlled lists for signed offline analysis and untouched retriever lists for target-free replication. On the four controlled blocks, fully disjoint correlations are weak or heterogeneous (-0.061 to 0.281), whereas reusing both probe views yields 0.600 to 0.718; all paired contrasts are large (0.436 to 0.661) and Holm-significant. The overlap effect is positive in all four untouched-list blocks. Increasing the probe from two to four views clearly improves disjoint reliability in only one block. Moreover, the probe predicts aggregation-movement magnitude (Spearman rho = 0.142 to 0.426) but not stable signed target benefit, and 7 of 12 fixed-fraction probe-routing points are strictly dominated at measured cost. Thus, when the intended estimand is predictive information about unseen perturbation behavior, validation targets must be observation-disjoint from the probe to isolate that information; signed utility and cost-sensitive decisions remain separate questions.

</details>
